### 3. 📊 当前状态
**刚做完**：收到 plan-001b-gpu0-v2 失败 cron 回调，完成初步诊断（vLLM EngineDeadError），已派诊断 Worker (worker_diagnose_plan-001b-gpu0-v2_20260514T015111) SSH 深查，已向 Director 汇报。

**Registry 关键状态**：
- plan-001b-gpu0-v2: DONE/failed — GPU0 上 7 tasks 的 episode collection，EngineCore 在 ep15 崩溃，部分数据可能已保存
- plan-001b-gpu1: ANALYZING/success — GPU1 上 4 tasks 完成（80 eps），find-non-living-thing 方向翻转（prescreening int>det 未复现）
- plan-001b-gpu1-extra: PREPARING — find-non-living-thing 追加 20 eps，有 pid 但状态未同步，存活状态不明
- plan-001b-gpu1-rebalance: PREPARING — GPU1 接管 GPU0 未完成 tasks 的计划，尚未执行
- plan-012-decomposition: DONE/success — 30 tasks 预分析 r=0.989 确认核心假设
- plan-011-rule-based: DONE/success — keyword heuristic 结构性失效

**等待中**：
- 诊断 Worker 回调 → 确认已收集数据量 + EngineCore 死因 + GPU1 进程状态 → 制定 v3 恢复方案
- worker_code_plan-005-bugfix 回调

**紧急**：两张 GPU 全部空闲 ⚠️

### 4. 📋 待办（条件触发式）
- 诊断 Worker 返回 → 根据已收集数据量决定：只重跑剩余 tasks（加 EngineCore 重试逻辑）还是全部重跑 → 派代码修复 Worker 给 episode_level_collection.py 加 vLLM 异常捕获+重启 → 立即在 GPU0 提交 v3
- plan-001b-gpu1-extra 确认死亡 → 在 GPU1 重新提交 find-non-living-thing extra 20 eps + plan-001b-gpu1-rebalance（freeze/chemistry-mix）
- plan-001b-gpu1-extra 确认存活 → 等其完成，然后 GPU1 启动 rebalance
- GPU0+GPU1 数据全部收齐 → 重跑 plan-012 decomposition 全量版（当前是 249 eps 预分析）
- plan-012 全量完成 → 启动 plan-002 router SFT + plan-005 routing pattern analysis
- Director 回复 C1 三档方案决策 → 更新 paper_framing.md

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
- **核心结论**：death avoidance 占 ~99% routing signal（Δ_full=+37.9, Δ_nd=+0.2, r=0.989）。论文贡献 = empirical finding + counterfactual methodology，不是 router 架构
- **Framing v3**（D013）：C1 = death avoidance dominance，C2 = methodology + diagnostic framework。Director 待决 C1 强度档（推荐 Tier B "~99% 主导"）
- **DDL**：EMNLP 2026 ARR short paper，2026-05-25（剩 11 天），关键路径 ~2 天实验 + ~3 天写作
- **易错点**：
  1. plan-001b-gpu1 的 find-non-living-thing 结果翻转（prescreening 5eps 显示 int>det，20eps 显示 tied）——这不影响 framing（D016 已确认 robust），但别把它当 internal-preferred 引用
  2. vLLM EngineCore 崩溃是本项目反复出现的问题，必须在代码层面加 try/except + engine 重建逻辑，光清理启动前孤儿不够
  3. plan-001b-gpu1-rebalance 的 spec 里写了多步命令，实际提交时要拆成单独的 submit_training_job 调用
  4. post_collection_analysis.sh、launch_plan002.sh、launch_plan004b.sh 已验证就绪（memory.md 记录），数据齐后可直接用
  5. plan-004b 依赖 plan-002 Step 1 (death_aware_labeling.py) 输出，不能完全并行

    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching.
